▶ apps-script-deploy — deploy state machine: new-project happy path
  ✔ first deploy walks create→upload→version→deploy in order and probes public access without re-fetching the deployment it just created (12.082875ms)
  ✔ every Apps Script API call carries the deploy token as a Bearer header — an unauthenticated call would 401 mid-deploy (0.978292ms)
  ✔ the uploaded manifest FORCES the public webapp config (ANYONE_ANONYMOUS / USER_DEPLOYING) regardless of what the repo manifest says — otherwise the probe can never pass (0.920125ms)
  ✔ a verified deploy persists the managed state as ready and marks the agent checklist webhookConfigured (0.69325ms)
  ✔ a verified deploy adopts the /exec URL (config patch + webhook field) and records stub_only — deploy proves wiring, never a real engine (0.809125ms)
  ✔ a corrupted local manifest aborts before ANY Google API traffic — no half-created project from a broken repo file (0.494792ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — deploy state machine: new-project happy path (16.201667ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — re-deploy reuses the managed project and deployment
  ✔ an existing managed state skips project creation and UPDATES the deployment in place — a POST would mint a duplicate web app with a NEW /exec URL (0.609833ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — re-deploy reuses the managed project and deployment (0.65075ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — deploy guards fail fast before any Google traffic
  ✔ a deploy already in flight is a no-op — no duplicate token prompts or racing API writes (0.383209ms)
  ✔ missing Sheet ID stops with a warning before requesting any token — there is nothing to attach the /exec URL to (3.81425ms)
  ✔ missing OAuth client ID stops with a warning before requesting any token (0.606792ms)
  ✔ an unsaved OAuth client change aborts the token request — deploying on the OLD client would mint a token for the wrong app (2.0435ms)
  ✔ GIS not loaded yet fails honestly with 'still loading' instead of hanging the deploy (1.717292ms)
  ✔ partially granted scopes abort the deploy — a weak token would fail half-way through with a confusing Google error (0.742625ms)
  ✔ a blocked permission popup maps to actionable 'allow popups' guidance, not a raw GIS error object (0.975792ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — deploy guards fail fast before any Google traffic (10.552625ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — public-access verification never saves an unverified /exec URL as usable
  ✔ a deployment that came back without ANYONE_ANONYMOUS access is persisted as needs_remediation and the webhook URL is NOT adopted (1.114583ms)
  ✔ a probe answered by the WRONG service (not the command-center stub) still counts as a failure — any 200 from a login wall must not pass (0.860917ms)
  ✔ a probe script load error (Google auth wall / 403 page) routes to probe remediation (0.817ms)
  ✔ a probe that never answers resolves via the armed timeout timer and routes to probe remediation — no infinite 'checking…' spinner (6.771625ms)
  ✔ the JSONP callback is removed from window even when the probe fails — leaked globals would collide on the next probe (0.865125ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — public-access verification never saves an unverified /exec URL as usable (10.618167ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — Google API errors map to actionable remediation, not raw payloads
  ✔ 'user has not enabled the Apps Script API' routes to Apps Script USER settings with the Google-provided link (1.968542ms)
  ✔ SERVICE_DISABLED routes to enabling script.googleapis.com in the OAuth client's Cloud project (0.577333ms)
  ✔ a 401 mid-deploy reads as an expired Google session with retry guidance (0.468167ms)
  ✔ a network/CORS failure against the Apps Script API reads as 'could not reach', not a raw TypeError (0.474208ms)
  ✔ any other API error keeps Google's own message as the detail and harvests at most two links as actions (0.499417ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — Google API errors map to actionable remediation, not raw payloads (4.092917ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — recheck re-probes public access without redeploying
  ✔ recheck is a no-op without a managed deploy state — there is nothing to probe and no status churn (0.502667ms)
  ✔ recheck re-reads the deployment from Google instead of trusting the cache — access fixed in the Apps Script editor must become visible (0.589167ms)
  ✔ a recheck that passes persists ready state, preserves lastDeployedAt, and adopts the URL with the recheck reason (0.503458ms)
  ✔ a recheck that still fails the probe keeps needs_remediation and does NOT adopt the URL (0.411041ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — recheck re-probes public access without redeploying (2.096709ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — SerpAPI callout probes only locally reachable workers

  ✔ a local worker is probed at /health with the query stripped, and a configured SerpAPI shows the configured badge (2.616958ms)
  ✔ a worker that does not report the flag is called out as too old instead of guessing yes/no (3.525125ms)
  ✔ an unreachable worker reads 'Worker unreachable' instead of leaving a stale badge or throwing (0.800708ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — SerpAPI callout probes only locally reachable workers (16.166708ms)
